# 跨國鎮壓 / Transnational Repression

**Tier E event record.** The community is a party to this: people of Taiwanese and Hong Kong
origin living in the United States, Canada and Europe are surveilled, coerced, or attacked on
behalf of the PRC state. This page is not commentary on the news — it is the archive's own
record of a pattern we have documented since 2021, assembled from our own holdings so that any
future story lands on a spine we already have.

## 從每日脈絡來的話

這頁不是今天的新聞。每日脈絡把你送到這裡，是因為今天的報導用了「跨國鎮壓」。點進來應看到兩件事：我們從 2021 年就有的紀錄，以及今天這幾則報導各自落在哪個已有的形狀上。下面每一則今天的消息都標 **報導**，不是我們核對過的檔案。

### 2026-10-13 — 報導，尚未成為我們的檔案

**報導。** 邱垂正把北京制裁台灣友邦的現任部長，稱為新型態跨國鎮壓，美國國務院公開反對這項脅迫。檔案裡已有他以陸委會副主委身分談反滲透法的發言（[陸委會：紅色滲透媒體須另檢討](../articles/taiwanjustice-net/2026/20260118114059_陸委會_紅色滲透媒體須另檢討_反滲透法不涉及_f2e812e84450157c.md)），沒有這次制裁的文件。形狀接近 2024 年已入檔的許穎婷遭懸賞：公開人物因為觸怒北京而被追究。證據等級不同。

**報導。** 卓榮泰就中國對台灣人跨境鎮壓、修反滲透法發言。檔案裡有他 2019 年在台大反送中之夜的紀錄（[挺台大人反送中](../articles/taiwanjustice-net/2022/20220929022324_2019_06_14_挺台大人反送中-卓榮泰_守護民主一點都不簡單_0968ae5adde60141.md)），沒有這次修法。

**報導。** 李明哲在歐洲巡講，提出民主國家是否該以「有罪推定」防範跨國鎮壓。檔案裡已有他的案子（[台灣救不了李明哲](../articles/taiwanjustice-net/2017/20171206223104_2017_12_01_台灣救不了李明哲-_陳茂雄-台灣時報社論-2017-12-2_d4717119c15afb17.md)）。巡講是新的，人不是。

**Reported.** 2026-10-13 — 《國有器官》在台放映屢遭恐嚇 陸委會：不容境外勢力剝奪民眾知情權 [大紀元](https://www.epochtimes.com/b5/26/10/13/n14869082.htm)
([wire](https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiZEFVX3lxTFByVUc0YlQzVVhRV3QzMUpic3docXhKTGp3Wm5ON1ItVWRfalhxMmxNa09YT2FqeHFYWnBlNDJla0xhcTk5TUZ3ZWZLR3poYUlOOUNTdXNrMTg0N0k5dGd3bGsyNVI?oc=5))
— 大紀元記者戴德蔓 2026-10-13. Same event as the LTN wire above. The Canadian documentary《國有器官》,
which exposes PRC forced organ harvesting through testimony of practitioners and victims' families,
drew bomb, gunshot and cyber threats against multiple Taiwan cinemas and related organizations during
its run; on 10-08 陸委會 invited the production team — director **章勇進** (Raymond Zhang), producer
**宋美馨** (Cindy Song), 台灣法輪大法學會理事長 **蕭松山**, 新唐人記者 **張東旭** — with 法政處長 **周鳴瑞**
receiving, to 關心 the intimidation and interference they faced. 周鳴瑞 framed the pattern as
跨國鎮壓: threats against screening venues and interference with distribution are aimed not only at
individuals but at the public's right to know. 宋美馨 said the pressure shows Beijing trying to "把這個
黑手伸進台灣"; the team thanked 警方 and government units for safety support during the screenings.
**Tier D** for the named subjects — 章勇進/宋美馨/蕭松山/張東旭/周鳴瑞 have no relationship or presence
in our archive (grep of content/ returns no person or org pages), so no pages minted; the community is
not a named party in this item, the theme is ours. Ruling consistent with needs-tier t_45219d6b.
The archive does hold the shape this event repeats: 2025-06-23 — 立法院外交及國防委員會邀請國安局、文化部、
警政署、陸委會報告「從『國有器官』在台播映困境看中國對台灣的長臂管轄策略，及我國的因應方案」
([陸委會：中國來台交流人士經過精心挑選](../articles/taiwanjustice-net/2025/20250623214457_陸委會_中國來台交流人士經過精心挑選-背景特別_1563c5e70a7bd23e.md)),
so the pattern is ours on file, not this year's headline.

carried on this spine: because 台灣 has no standalone 跨境鎮壓罪, 律師 argue the gap be closed either by
amending 《反滲透法》 to reach rights-infringing repression of individuals, or by creating a new
**未申報外國政府代理人罪** modeled on 18 U.S.C. §951 — the "undeclared foreign-government agent" offense
the article uses as its comparison class (and which our own archive already carries in practice: 莫伊尼安
案, [2022-06-23](../articles/taiwanjustice-net/2022/20220627115741_2022_06_23_前美軍飛行員認罪_承認向中國提供航空信息並收_d74b2cdec47dd746.md),
and the Harvard 千人計畫 case, [2020-01-28](../articles/taiwanjustice-net/2020/20200206151652_2020_01_28_中國千人計畫踢鐵板-哈佛大學3人被控暗助中國_生_58777e44d3b70d88.md)).
Named subjects: **賴又豪** and **陳思妤**, the two 律師 researching 本土跨國鎮壓 cases (same pair credited
by 報導者 on the same analysis), plus 陸委會 as the agency quoted. A grep of the vault finds no
relationship or presence for either lawyer (zero hits both names); 陸委會 and 邱垂正 are held, but as the
cited agency, not as parties to this item. The community is not a named party here — the story is about
Taiwan's legal gap, not about a person we record — **the theme is ours**: the cases the lawyers cite as
evidence of that gap (the 在台跨國鎮壓 cases now 偵結起訴, and the surveillance-of-family pattern) are the
Tier E record this spine already carries. No pages minted.
**Note:** the earlier ruling on this card was made without the article body (Google News redirect is
JS-only, IA CDX offline that run) and named speculative subjects. The body is now read (2026-10-14, via
the live redirect to rti.org.tw/news?pid=237533); the ruling stands, corrected to the subjects actually
named above.

### 2026-10-17 — 報導，尚未成為我們的檔案

**Reported.** 2026-10-05 — DOJ says 'Chinese foreign agent' charged with surveilling Taiwanese president's family [ABC News](https://abcnews.com/Politics/doj-chinese-foreign-agent-charged-surveilling-taiwanese-presidents/story?id=137014898)
— needs-tier t_7979cbe0. Luke Barr, ABC News, 2026-10-05 19:14. ABC's account of the DOJ announcement of the *United States v. Wanying Zhang* case already carried on this spine: the 2026-10-04 arrest at LAX, the 2026-10-05 initial appearance (no plea, remanded), and the complaint's surveillance allegations. No new facts, no new subjects.

**Tier ruling.** **Tier E** for the named subjects — Wanying Zhang, Lai Ching-te, Lai Ting-yu — because the community is a party to the underlying event (a sitting president's family surveilled on U.S. soil, families surveilled here), and all three already hold pages with documented ties that predate this news ([people/wanying-heather-zhang](../people/wanying-heather-zhang.md), [people/lai-ching-te](../people/lai-ching-te.md), [people/lai-ting-yu](../people/lai-ting-yu.md)). No pages minted; the tie is shown, not minted. **The card's premise ("our archive records no relationship or presence for them") is inaccurate** — this is the same case already ruled Tier E under needs-tier t_83a964c2 (2026-10-14), and the case record on wanying-heather-zhang predates this article.

**Reporting-quality note:** ABC's article states the target was "Taiwanese President Tsai Ing-wen's son" — a misattribution; Tsai Ing-wen has no children. Law-enforcement sources (CBS, AP, NYT, Al Jazeera, Time, Fox) identify the target as **Lai Ting-yu (賴廷與)**, eldest son of President **Lai Ching-te**, and the DOJ press release itself says only "a high-ranking Taiwanese government official." The article is cited with that flag and registered on the EN-press line of wanying-heather-zhang.

— needs-tier t_7cabe83d. An AI-curated cluster of 11 wire items (鏡週刊 cover story, 公視, 自由, Newtalk, 三立, 中時, 鏡報, 太報, Yahoo即時) on the CCP cross-border-repression intervention in the 2026 Taipei mayoral race. The cluster is not itself a source; its substantive new content — the family-sanction thread (2026-10-13 港媒點名 VWAY → 10-14 國台辦懲戒 → 10-15 安保協會時間軸 → 10-16 陸委會介選定性) — had **no prior record in the vault** (沈土城／兆億／VWAY: zero hits) and is now minted as [t-中國介選鎖定沈伯洋父親](t-中國介選鎖定沈伯洋父親-安保協會揭-港媒抹黑-國台辦制裁-時間軸-家族企業二度遭懲戒.md).

**Tier ruling.** **Tier E** for 沈伯洋 — page exists ([people/沈伯洋](../people/沈伯洋.md)), tie predates the news, precedent ruling t_af09d600; the event sits on this Tier E spine (surveillance prosecution already recorded 2026-10-07). **Tier D** for every other named subject — 沈土城、鄭永津、鞠覺賢、林慶、黃逸豪、張育萌 zero vault hits; 梁文傑、矢板明夫、何韻詩、林榮基 commentator-citations only — cited in the new wire page, no pages minted for them. **The card's premise ("our archive records no relationship or presence for them") is inaccurate for the primary subject** — the 沈伯洋 tie is recorded in the vault and predates this cluster; it is accurate for the family members, whose株連 is recorded as reported allegation, not biography. AI-summary caveat: the cluster's 重點摘要 states 中共指使 as fact; the underlying reporting (and the 安保協會 report itself) labels the 港媒→國台辦 sequence as pattern analysis and the trade data as unverified — the minted page carries the labeled version.

### 2026-10-18 — 報導，尚未成為我們的檔案

**Reported.** 2026-10-17 — 【新聞週刊】法輪功學員多倫多遭襲 加議員譴責暴力 促追責 - 新唐人電視台
([wire](https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiYEFVX3lxTE94aDkweGY4b3VyMExKaGlQYTV5TEgyZ2J4OXNaNjBmN3JFcmc5cWdsRE1iWWdGdmF6TjM2U2Q2d0hzZUZoTXAtLWlRSHNwV0R6ZGRVR2h2UGRwUVlZdHBmX9IBZkFVX3lxTFBtTGN3aG9aaEhjZXJwckdEb1ZCbWpFOWhrMjA1YzRDanNnbnRRazBXQjRFLUh4UHlmVl9Ja0FVeXlma0xBMEdiT1VFZ1FTVTVSUDZmclQzWllUTkZfRlI3VGxkam9tUQ?oc=5),
[NTD](https://www.ntdtv.com/b5/2026-10-17/a104142798.html)) — needs-tier t_c0953057. The weekly wrap of the
2026-09-22 assault on Falun Gong practitioners on the public sidewalk outside the PRC consulate in Toronto: a man
in a jacket bearing the PRC flag smashed the truth-point's display boards, pushed practitioner **李娜 (Lina Li)**,
62, leaving her chin bleeding, attempted to kick **耿 (Nora Geng)** as she filmed him, told Li 「我用反分裂法，
我可以槍斃妳」 and claimed the sidewalk was 「中國領土」. The item's substance is the 10-13 cross-party response:
Conservative **Jacob Mantle** wrote to Foreign Affairs Minister **Anita Anand** demanding an investigation and the
attacker's expulsion if he proves a Chinese diplomat or foreign agent, joined by **Michael Cooper**, **Judy Sgro**
(co-chair, Parliamentary Friends of Falun Gong), **Alexis Brunelle-Duceppe** and **Jenny Kwan**, who said Canada has
no legislation to tackle transnational repression. Toronto police said the matter is under investigation; the
consulate denied involvement. **Alleged, not established:** any link between the attacker and the consulate remains
an open police question, and the CSIS/公共安全部 finding that the CCP is Canada's largest foreign-interference actor
is the MPs' characterization, not a court finding.

**Tier ruling.** **Tier D — cite reporting, no page mint.** The event is on **Canadian** soil; the subjects are
Canadian Falun Gong practitioners and Canadian parliamentarians; the item carries no U.S.-soil targeting and no
families-surveilled-here claim. Same discriminator as the Canadian leg of this theme under needs-tier t_63ba370b
(C-219, 2026-10-14): repression aimed at diaspora *in Canada* is this spine's theme, not a party claim by this
community. No pages minted.

**Card premise — partially inaccurate.** The card states the archive records no relationship or presence for the
named subjects; for three of them it does, so the tie is shown rather than minted:
- **Michael Cooper** (古柏, 保守黨, 亞伯達) — a documented 友台 legislator in our own holdings: he sponsored the
  「加台關係架構法」 (Canada-Taiwan Relations Framework Act), passed first reading with a House ovation
  ([2025-07-18](../articles/taiwanjustice-net/2025/20250718105536_加拿大議員提出_加台關係架構法_國會一讀通_27e9c74d0cb15a62.md)),
  and urged the Trudeau government to 「在外交和貿易上擁抱台灣」 with a 台加 FIPA and CPTPP support
  ([2026-02-09](../articles/taiwanjustice-net/2026/20260209120944_加拿大議員古柏再挺台_籲杜魯道政府_擁抱台灣_ba64fa83219ec21c.md)).
  His recorded tie is to **Canada–Taiwan relations**, not to this assault.
- **Judy Sgro** (史葛洛, 自由黨) — as chair of 加台國會議員友好協會 she announced its accession as co-chair of the
  歐洲福爾摩沙俱樂部 ([2025-09-15](../articles/taiwanjustice-net/2025/20250915162821_歐洲福爾摩沙俱樂部擴大實力_新添加拿大生力軍_7a93c6ec87d95825.md)),
  and is cited in the 2025-10 加國議員訪台 coverage
  ([2025-10-13](../articles/taiwanjustice-net/2025/20251013084934_加拿大_國會議員訪台不應被中國當成侵略藉口_90f9267a9ee6a771.md)).
  Same characterization: a Canada–Taiwan tie, not a party to this event.
- **Jenny Kwan** (關慧貞) — two vault hits, both as a quoted commentator (2021-09-13
  [加拿大聯邦大選-屢見紅色勢力影響干預](../articles/taiwanjustice-net/2021/20211025213715_2021_09_13_加拿大聯邦大選-屢見紅色勢力影響干預_f2163520421cd041.md),
  and the 2026 人權體系 piece) → commentator-citation, 報導-only.
- **曾厚仁** — 18 vault hits as 駐加/駐歐代表 in CNA reporting; here he is the author of the The Hub op-ed
  「加拿大不應容忍中國的對外脅迫行徑」 on this case. Named official/commentator, not a community party.
- **余厚強** — three vault hits, all name-mentions in index/listing pages (2016-01-31 letter to 美國政府國會和聯合國
  for 于世文 alongside 陳破空 and 劉俊國, and as a 中國民運全球網路大會 speaker). No person page and no relationship
  record; a listing hit is not a tie.
**Commentator-citations, not zero hits** (re-probed with simplified-character variants after an English-only
grep returned false zeros — the t_88e678ef lesson): **吳紹平** is quoted twice in our holdings as 旅美人權律師
(2025-09-17 器官移植 and 暴力防疫 pieces) — a same-name lawyer, not this item's practitioner, and a quoted
source either way; **公子沈** is quoted as a YouTuber on the 2025-04-30 YouTube 黃標 piece; **Anita Anand** is

**Genuine zero hits** (both scripts): Mantle, 利松/Wladyslaw Lizon, Alexis Brunelle-Duceppe, 鄭敬基/Joe Tay,
Jeff Lee, Maria Cheung (this spine page's own C-219 note is the only mention), 潘學峰, 侯世軍, 李炳嚴, 葛開英,

**Why the theme is ours — on file before this headline.** The archive carried the consulate-front shape first:
2023-06-01, 加拿大警方稱已關閉與「中國警察站」有關的非法警務活動
([sourced](../articles/taiwanjustice-net/2023/20230922225159_2023_06_01_加拿大警方稱已關閉與中國警察站有關的非_77a87ed49e934a1d.md))
— physical PRC policing on Canadian soil; and 洛杉磯法輪大法學會 stands as co-signatory #24 of the 2022-08-11
global 連署 for 高智晟失蹤五週年, the 洛杉磯中領館前 action

### 2026-10-19 — 報導，尚未成為我們的檔案

**Reported.** 2026-10-19 — US indicts California woman accused of spying on Taiwan president's son for China [NBC News](https://www.nbcnews.com/news/us-news/california-woman-indicted-charges-spying-china-rcna604242) — needs-tier t_35ee8f07 (syndicated wire headline of the same NBC article triaged under t_bca025f2 the same hour). David Douglas & Dennis Romero, NBC News, 2026-10-18 20:58 EDT. The *United States v. Wanying Zhang* case already carried on this spine advances to **indictment posture**: an indictment was filed **2026-10-09** in the U.S. District Court, Central District of California, charging **conspiracy and unregistered-foreign-agent** offenses (18 U.S.C. § 951), the allegations mirroring the 2026-10-03 complaint; maximum penalty reported at **15 years**. The indictment describes the handler as an unnamed Chinese government official and former classmate at her alma mater, China's **University of International Relations**, which the FBI affidavit calls "closely affiliated with China's intelligence apparatus"; an example tasking is the request for pictures "of the house" where the son lives. Magistrate Judge Steve Kim ordered **pretrial detention** after the 2026-10-07 hearing; post-indictment arraignment set **Friday 2026-10-23**. Defense counsel Craig Harbaugh, in court 2026-10-07: the government's "sleeper agent for the last 10 years" theory "is not supported." PRC Foreign Ministry spokesman **郭嘉昆** said he was "not familiar" and Beijing "opposes vilifying China by peddling the so-called 'Chinese spy' narrative."

**Tier ruling.** **Tier E** for the named primary subjects — Wanying Zhang, Lai Ching-te, Lai Ting-yu: the community is a party to the underlying event (a sitting president's family surveilled on U.S. soil at PRC direction; families surveilled here — the card's own Tier E test), and all three already hold pages with documented pre-news ties ([people/wanying-heather-zhang](../people/wanying-heather-zhang.md), [people/lai-ching-te](../people/lai-ching-te.md), [people/lai-ting-yu](../people/lai-ting-yu.md)). **No pages minted — tie shown, not minted.** The card's premise ("our archive records no relationship or presence for them") is **inaccurate** for the primary subjects; this is the fourth triage of the same case on this spine (t_83a964c2, t_7979cbe0, t_bca025f2). **Tier D** for the new secondary subjects, cited only: **Craig Harbaugh** (defense counsel) zero vault hits; **郭嘉昆** 3 vault hits, all as quoted 中國外交部發言人 (2025-03-19, 2025-05-18, 2025-11-17) — commentator-citation, not a party; **UIR/國際關係學院** — the vault's 5 "國際關係學院" hits are all different institutions (中國人民大學, 荷蘭, NTU 拉惹勒南), false positives, no independent record. **One date corrected** against a second source: the companion "Individual 1" fled to China in late **September 2026** (NBC's 2026-10-07 detention-hearing report: prosecutors said he "had already fled to China two weeks ago"), not September 2025 as first entered from the indictment article's bare "September." Allegation language preserved throughout; indictment = allegation, presumption of innocence stands.

([NTD](https://www.ntdtv.com/b5/2026/10/19/a104143378.html)) — needs-tier t_50d337d5. 新唐人電視台 Washington interview, reporter 菲奧娜·吉, dated 2026-10-20 03:31 BJT. **Body read in full**, both the 10-20 republication (`a104143378`) and NTD's own original (`a104138927`, 2026-10-04/05): the text is identical word-for-word, and only the dateline, the tag set and the framing clause 「在近期召開川習會之際」 differ — so this card is a **re-airing of the same interview**, not a new event. Substance: Epoch Times exclusively obtained a 《涉政落查人員庫》 from the PRC 政法系統 — **24,170 records, ~10,000+ people** after dedup — which tracks CCP-watched accounts on overseas platforms (X, Facebook, 大紀元, 新唐人, 文學城) and resolves them to national ID, address, work unit and family; ~22,500 records (93%) carry a mainland location and, of the ~10,500 with a domestic residence, **4,783 are pinned to a door-address** — the article itself notes a China address may be a pre-departure or family address, not current presence. 大紀元 states it **cannot independently verify the database's overall accuracy**; reporters preliminarily matched some public figures' entries against reality. Named targets: **魏京生、陳光誠、盛雪**; named groups: 法輪功, 大紀元/新唐人 staff, 神韻 artists, and left-wing student/worker activists. The article frames the leak through *our* case — the 2026-10-04 FBI arrest of **張婉瑩** at LAX and the 2026-10-05 DOJ announcement (charges plus a 40-person denaturalization track, including **李平**, a convicted man who collected 法輪功 intelligence for Beijing) — and, per CBS's review of court files, ties her to 太子集團 key figure **陳星 (Chen Sokly)**. 盛雪's own testimony (to 大紀元): three entries under 四川/甘肅/加拿大; 1996 and 2008 entry refusals; 2013–2017 inescapable attack-groups targeting her husband, sister, uncle and brother; 2025-04 impersonation threats issued **in her name** to a Vancouver 神韻 theatre and to 總統府, 桃園/松山機場, police stations and 台北101; PEN America records her 2019 identity theft and continuing threats. 評論員**李錚鳴** (not on the list, per himself) recounts 公檢法 visits to his mainland relatives. **Labeling:** everything about the database is 大紀元's claim; everything about 張婉瑩/李平/盧建旺 is US prosecutorial **allegation** — indictment = allegation, presumption of innocence stands; 盛雪's account is her attributed testimony.

**Tier ruling.** The item sits **on this Tier E spine** — its narrative frame is the community's own case (a sitting president's son surveilled on U.S. soil at MSS direction; families surveilled here, the card's own Tier E test) — but its named targets are **them, not us**: mainland dissidents, a faith group, independent-media staff and artists. — needs-tier t_c7017d89. 中時新聞網記者戴志揚 2026-10-20 15:49. **Body read in full** (the Google News redirect was again inaccessible; read from the live 中時 URL resolved by search — ruling on read text, the t_88e678ef rule). Substance: the 立法院外交及國防委員會 invited 國安局局長蔡明彥 et al. to present 「國家情報工作暨國家安全局業務報告」 on 10-22; per the 國安局's written report delivered to 立院, the PRC directs 境外及在台協力者 to 監視及跟拍 特定國人 to capture private information as 威脅籌碼; to send 恐嚇郵件 under the names of 反中人士 to damage targets' reputations; and to engineer 肢體衝突、潑漆討債 and 假車禍 — violence aimed at 寒蟬效應. The report adds its own statistics: 共諜起訴 (excluding 《反滲透法》 cases) 6 persons in 2020 → 73 in 2026; 179 adjudicated, 163 convicted (91%); average sentence 1 year (2020) → 6 years 6 months (2026); since 2020, 151 中資未經核准落地營運/在台挖角 cases and 50 科技竊密 cases broken; 71 反滲透法 indictments during the 2024 二合一 election; 「促融促統」 forums targeting 青年族群及工商企業. **Every figure and characterization is the 國安局 report's, attributed:** these are government statistics and allegations delivered to the legislature, not court findings; nothing here is recorded as established fact.

**Reported.** 2026-10-21 — 【禁聞】中共跨國鎮壓鎖定逾萬人 美加反制 [wire](https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiYEFVX3lxTE1NcnJOcmNkaHotelRFNFRzbHluM2RKMDJoUWVJMUpIa3FYZ0Z3blBPMXRLNDJWYzFKYmQ2T0t1Vm5MT0JBVE5kUjRaZnlyb3pkYzVqWTlBOGcxZW02MTh5Y9IBZkFVX3lxTE5MZnBMRGluMk56R0RGLXFPbFdvUWh1eWZ3bWNnZTVzTEtCY3E2WnZXLTVucVVGTG5OVFE4cGVKUW9sTVVNeDUyZm5BYzUxdFd3cTBtREY5Q0JEMUZtTFlrMUxCYXdVdw?oc=5)
([NTD b5](https://www.ntdtv.com/b5/2026/10/21/a104143996.html)) — needs-tier t_c53fb57f. 新唐人電視台【禁聞】segment, 北京時間 2026-10-22 01:56, `a104143996`, 編輯/採訪 李韻、後製 鐘元. **Body read in full** from the live NTD b5 URL (the Google News redirect returned "Content was inaccessible or not found" — the t_88e678ef read-text rule; URL resolved by search). **This is the fourth publisher-side filing of ONE story** — the 《涉政落查人員庫》 大紀元 exclusive (`n14872026`, 記者冷鋒), already triaged on this spine as t_e58ab73c (大紀元 original), t_8dbfccc6 (NTD condensed rewrite `a104143841`) and, via that ruling, NTD's verbatim syndication `a104143646`. This copy is the **【禁聞】 TV wrap**: the database core is restated, not advanced (《涉政落查人員庫》 from the PRC 政法系統, 24,170 records / ~10,000+ people after dedup, 大紀元 itself states it cannot independently verify overall accuracy; overseas-platform accounts on X / Facebook / 大紀元 / 新唐人 / 文學城 resolved to real name, ID, address, work unit and family; ~22,500 records (93%) carry a mainland location and of the ~10,500 with a domestic residence, 4,783 are pinned to a door-address, with the reporting's own caveat that a China address may be pre-departure or a family address). **What is new in this copy** is not the database but the two interview blocks and one piece of colour: 界立建 (called 「中國民主黨國際聯盟主席」 here) says 「一萬多人的統計數據，只少不多」, that 大數據 monitoring now sweeps 民運領袖、活動參與者、自媒體網紅 and 「意識形態能帶給大家覺醒的倡導者」, that the 「海外反賊名單」 and 「漢奸展覽館」 sites look 民間牽頭 but their 技術、網路支持、經費資金 are 「背後肯定是中共國家安全部去統一撥款」, that 納粹 and 恐怖分子 alike observe 「禍不及家人」 whereas this practice 「株連、軟禁受害者國內的親人」 makes it 「跨國恐怖犯罪」 deserving 馬格尼茨基 accountability — and the item reports that **he has submitted evidence of CCP 跨國迫害 to the FBI and to Congress**. 盛雪 (called 「民主陣線副主席」 here) gives the 統戰-system framing: the apparatus predates the PRC, its core method is 鎖定目標人物 and collecting all their information so that 軟肋、日常生活、關係網、朋友、家人 become 「打擊對手的槓桿」; it grew 「更加激進蠻橫」 after 習近平 took office, seeding 線人 across sectors; and the 退出黨團隊 campaign is a way to remove the system's 著力點. The item also attributes to her 偽造色情圖片, 冒名發布炸彈威脅 and 連坐 persecution of her mainland relatives. **Every characterization above is the speaker's, attributed:** no indictment, filing, or court finding is cited, and nothing here is recorded as established fact. The closing 反制 roll-call (加拿大 C-219 三讀, 美參院《制止跨國鎮壓法案》, DOJ 優先事項) restates legislation already carried on this spine.

**Tier ruling.** **Tier D — cite reporting, no page mint.** The community is not a party to this item: it names no 台美人 target, records no U.S.-soil action against any person or organization we hold, and makes no families-surveilled-here claim about the community. ### 2026-10-23 — 報導，尚未成為我們的檔案

**Reported.** 2026-10-23 — Suspected Chinese spy pleads not guilty to targeting Taiwan leader’s kin [wire](https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMipgFBVV95cUxNT2dkdTFYRlFJalRaSngyYmZJSEgwUUd5N2QyWUk4NE5oUENTUmQxRVRDc3l4SWJ0NnBLWnNsSllMSEdfNS0tX0phQUcyQV9qTGVpMUU3cWNvTFd1VXU4Z1JQeHpxYUc3cUtMVk9ZODdUUGFwUUxsY1dxWTF1TmhoeTVmQVhRQlh0M3ZHaGR1QVkwUl9iTkVyd1pzaVJGemN1eG14eEJR?oc=5)
([INQUIRER.net US Bureau](https://usa.inquirer.net/213391/suspected-chinese-spy-pleads-not-guilty-to-targeting-taiwan-leaders-kin)) — needs-tier t_356d22dc. **The 7th triage of ONE case on this spine** — *United States v. Wanying Zhang, aka "Heather"* (18 U.S.C. § 951), already carried under t_83a964c2 (2026-10-14), t_7979cbe0 (10-17), t_bca025f2 + t_35ee8f07 (10-19), t_2a804da6 (10-20), t_928b9374 (10-20). The Google News redirect is inaccessible (t_88e678ef read-text rule), and INQUI's own URL served a Cloudflare interstitial (`Just a moment...`, 5.8 KB) while The Online Citizen 403'd, so the body was read in full from **CBS News** (Josh Boswell, CBS News Investigative Unit, 2026-10-23 5:15 PM EDT, `cbsnews.com/news/wanying-zhang-alleged-chinese-agent-pleads-not-guilty/`) and **NBC Los Angeles** (Helen Jeong, Danielle Smith & AP, 2026-10-23, `nbclosangeles.com/news/local/irvine-spy-china-fbi-lax/3960062/`), which carry the same event and the same quotes as the INQUI wire. Ruling made on read text, not on the wire headline. **What this filing advances is the court posture, and only the court posture:** Zhang **pleaded not guilty** on Friday **2026-10-23** at the post-indictment arraignment in federal court in downtown Los Angeles — the step our pages carried as *scheduled*, now held. She appeared in a white jumpsuit and shackles, speaking only to deny the charges. The judge set a **pretrial hearing for 2026-11-12** and **trial to begin 2026-11-30** at the federal courthouse in **Santa Ana**, estimated to last **four days** — dates that were not in the archive before this filing. Both prosecutors and defense counsel **Craig Harbaugh** declined to comment. Husband **Edric Ding** (realtor at the same Irvine firm) had appeared at earlier hearings and did not appear Friday, after prosecutors claimed in a recent filing that Zhang was having an affair with the man who accompanied her on the alleged Washington-state surveillance; CBS again names him as **Fatai Miao**, not charged, who left Southern California on a one-way ticket to China, and whose son denies any wrongdoing. NBC/AP add the Presidential Office statement attributed by name to spokesperson **Karen Kuo** (「a classic case of transnational repression」, 「Taiwan condemns in the strongest terms such barbaric acts…」) and the Chinese Foreign Ministry's reply (not aware of the incident; opposes 「hyped-up accusations of 'China spying'」). **Labeling unchanged:** indictment = allegation, no finding of fact, defendant presumed innocent until proven guilty (推定無罪); every characterization above is the speaker's own, attributed.

**Tier ruling. Tier E reaffirmed for the named primary subjects — Wanying Zhang, Lai Ching-te, Lai Ting-yu — 0 pages minted, tie shown not minted.** Verified against the live pages this attempt: [people/wanying-heather-zhang](../people/wanying-heather-zhang.md) (naturalized U.S. citizen, Irvine CA realtor, defendant), [people/lai-ting-yu](../people/lai-ting-yu.md) (sourced father relation via `sources/zh-wikipedia-lai-ching-te.md`; Seattle-area U.S.-resident engineer; the person surveilled), [people/lai-ching-te](../people/lai-ching-te.md). The community is a **party to the underlying event** on the card's own Tier E limbs: a sitting president's family surveilled **on U.S. soil** (Seattle suburb, Sept 2025) at alleged PRC direction, the defendant resident in Irvine, the prosecution in a U.S. federal court, and the FBI itself framing it as transnational repression. The qualifying pages already carry the Community Relevance line, so nothing is minted. **The card's premise — "our archive records no relationship or presence for them" — is INACCURATE as stated for the primary subjects**; this is the 6th correction of that premise on this spine, and the ruling does not depend on it either way.

**Subject-by-subject probes re-derived from disk this run** (traditional **and** simplified variants plus romanization — the t_88e678ef false-zero rule; live counts, not carried from prior cards):
- **Wanying Zhang / 張婉瑩 / 張萬英?** — 張婉瑩 8 files, 張萬英 2, `Wanying` 4, `賴廷與` 7, `Lai Ting-yu` 5, `苗發泰` 2, `Fatai` 2, `Craig Harbaugh` 2, `Essayli` 1, `Kevin Holt` 1 — **every one with 0 hits in `articles/`**, i.e. our own case record and the digest's wire pages, not third-party archive text. Person pages exist for Zhang, Lai Ting-yu, Lai Ching-te. **Tier E, tie shown.**
- **Edric Ding** (husband, named in this filing) — **a substring collision, not presence.** `Edric` returns 27 files but they are Dr. **Edric**k (×2, `people/liao-shu-zong.md`), C**edric** Yang (×23) and our own Zhang page; `Edric Ding` occurs only on `people/wanying-heather-zhang.md`. **No person page → Tier D**, cited only: he is a realtor at the same Irvine firm and appeared at earlier hearings, but nothing in the record makes him a party to anything the community was part of, and he is not charged.
- **Karen Kuo** (總統府 spokesperson, named only in the NBC/AP copy) — **genuine zero** (`Karen Kuo` 0, both scripts of 郭雅慧 tested separately below). Quoted official of the ROC presidential office → **Tier D**, no page (t_83a964c2 Chen Binhua precedent).
- **郭雅慧** (the Presidential Office spokesperson our own TJJ holdings name in other contexts) — 23 files, **21 in `articles/`, 2 quoted lines inside other people's pages** (`people/lai-ching-te.md` 2023-11-16 競辦發言人, `people/bi-khim-hsiao.md` 2025-07-07 總統府), **no person page** → quoted-official presence ≠ tie (Rubio shape, t_50d337d5). **Tier D.** Note the two spokesperson names are not reconciled here: this filing attributes the statement to Karen Kuo, our archive has 郭雅慧 in the 總統府/競辦 spokesperson role in 2023–2025 — flagged, not adopted.
- **Fatai Miao / 苗發泰 (phonetic)** — Tier D **reaffirmed** (t_928b9374 finding, not re-litigated): 2 files, both ours; vault `Miao` hits are unrelated persons (Yi-Miao Huang, Yu-Miao Wen, Kurt Miao); **not charged**, an FBI person of interest; NY Post's single-source 「lover」 framing still not recorded, and the affair claim here stays attributed to the prosecutors' filing.

- **Publishers, not subjects** — **INQUIRER.net / 華人今日網 / 台灣時報焦點新聞** and **民視新聞網**: the digest seeded two uncommitted wire pages for this same headline at 20:30 and 20:36 today (`topics/t-涉監控賴清德兒子爾灣華女不認罪-台灣時報焦點-新聞-華人今日網.md`, `topics/t-中共特工-跟監賴清德兒子-下場慘了-驚傳拒認罪-最重刑期曝光-民視新聞網.md`). **Why the theme is ours — on file before this headline.** The archive carried the U.S.-soil, families-in-the-frame shape before this cycle made it a headline: the 2022-08-11 洛杉磯法輪大法學會 co-signature of the global 高智晟失蹤五週年 連署 at the 洛杉磯中領館前, the 2002-10-22 大芝加哥台灣同鄉會 round-the-clock protests at Jiang Zemin's Chicago hotel, and the 2023-06-01 Toronto 中國警察站 closure — all sourced above from our own holdings, none news-derived. The Zhang case is the first prosecution of its kind to reach a sitting president's family on American soil, and it is recorded as **event history** with allegation language preserved, to be updated as the case moves (pretrial 2026-11-12 → trial 2026-11-30).

### 2026-10-22 — 報導，尚未成為我們的檔案

**Reported.** 2026-10-22 — 中國民主黨國際聯盟揭中共20多宗跨國鎮壓 [大紀元](https://www.epochtimes.com/b5/26/10/22/n14874264.htm)
([wire](https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiYkFVX3lxTE9HNDd6MGRBM3ZDQldtNmU4aFllZjFnMUlDTk5nUVk2d0tBV2draGtTeVkzMEhtQWh6bld5VUF6T1hJakZVMl9feVc3aEZqT1hRcmpkQmxsVExja09oYWhfZEhB0gFnQVVfeXFMT0gxODU3dXFsV0pmTDRVWnZqSkZOcV9DWm5RM2pfLVhEV0JTRm5NdWtJT2VwX1p4cGk3Mmh6M2ZIeEs0UDBfNVlLTXpHLU52WTF0LVNXZUdENXBUQ2xlc3YwTUd0Q0Fybw?oc=5))
— 大紀元記者陶若水紐約報導 2026-10-22 09:24 AM, `n14874264`, 責任編輯 鄭樺. **Body read in full** from the live
epochtimes b5 URL (the Google News redirect is JS-only — the t_88e678ef read-text rule; URL resolved by search, and
the 阿波羅新聞網 syndication carries the identical text, author 陶若水, 責任編輯 李華 — publisher-side editor drift, not
a text variant). **This is NOT a sibling of the 《涉政落查人員庫》 cluster** already on this spine under
t_e58ab73c / t_8dbfccc6 / t_c53fb57f: that story is the leaked 24,170-record database; this one is a **street
action** — 中國民主黨國際聯盟 (CDPIA) held a rally on **2026-10-18 in front of the Queens Library in Flushing**
(王后區法拉盛圖書館前), a 華人-concentrated public venue, with display boards showing photographs of Chinese police
visiting members' families, footage of the assault on 民運人士 at the earlier Washington protest, and phone/微信
threat recordings played over a loudspeaker to passers-by.

**What the item alleges (all attributed; no finding of fact).** 主席 **界立建** says that around the September 2026
visit to the U.S. by 習近平 and the organization's Washington protest, the alliance holds evidence of **20+ cases**
of intimidation, threats and harassment against members and their families; some were threatened before departure,
gave up protesting, and were harassed anyway. Named first-person accounts: **徐其仁** (江西) supplied a call recording
in which local 國保 personnel, having taken his mother to a 中共社區機構 and phoning him in front of her, said they
knew of his CDPIA membership and his dealings with 界立建, demanded he withdraw, referenced his pregnant wife, warned
his mother could be implicated and that he faced imprisonment if he returned to China. **張劉敏** (河南鄭州) and
**方碩** (江蘇) each received threat calls before the Washington trip — 張劉敏 threatened with her child's safety —
both stood down, were harassed regardless, and were told to leave the alliance and cut contact with 界立建 or
「後果自負」; 張劉敏 said she would not withdraw and would publicize the case and pursue legal accountability.
界立建 said the harassment is not confined to families in China: the alliance's **Malaysia** web administrator
**楊佳** was followed and threatened and dare not return to his residence, and members in **Thailand** feared
rendition to China. Rally attendee **李中平** said Flushing was chosen precisely because it is 華人集中, and called
on the U.S. government to investigate CCP agents operating illegally in the U.S. and to protect threatened people.
界立建 said the alliance is collecting names, recordings and video to submit to the U.S. government, Congress and
international human-rights bodies under the Global Magnitsky Act, and urged targets to report and preserve evidence.

**Tier ruling — Tier D for every named subject; no page minted.** The CONTROL §1b discriminator is party status, and
it returns Tier D here even though the venue is a U.S.-soil 華人 public space and the theme is genuinely ours:
the people named as **targets** are members of a **Chinese-dissident** organization, their surveilled families are
in **mainland China** (江西, 河南鄭州, 江蘇), and the two cross-border legs are **Malaysia** and **Thailand** — not
台美人 subjects, not families surveilled here. The U.S.-soil element in this item is the **rally**, not the
repression. No 台美人 target, no action against any person or organization we record, no families-surveilled-here
claim. Precedents: t_c53fb57f (界立建 as quoted interviewee), t_c0953057 / t_63ba370b (non-U.S.-soil legs of this
theme), t_50d337d5 / t_c143aa9a (no pages for unnamed groups).

**Subject probes (both scripts + romanization, t_88e678ef rule).**

  mobile for 中國民主黨／中國民主平臺 actions at 好萊塢中國劇院 2021-10-31 and 洛杉磯中領館前 2023-01-07, 圖文提供 credits

## Why this belongs in Echopedia

Per CONTROL §1b, the Tier D↔E discriminator is whether **the community is a party**, not
whether the story is famous. It is:

- The targets are members of, or adjacent to, the diaspora we record — 台灣人 abroad, 僑界,
  Hong Kong residents in U.S. cities, and family members of public figures.
- The operations occur on soil where our members live: Los Angeles, the San Gabriel Valley,
  Toronto, university towns across the U.S.
- Our archive carried the evidence **before** the current cycle made it a headline.

Labeling standard: every claim below carries its status. **Sourced** = a document in this
archive. **Reported** = press assertion we have not independently verified. **Alleged** =
accusation in a filing or statement, recorded with attribution and without a finding of fact.
Nothing is omitted for being uncomfortable; nothing is asserted beyond what the record holds.

News redirect is JS-only and curl returns only the Google News shell — the t_88e678ef read-text rule). **Form matters
for the ruling: this is an opinion column, not a news report.** The writer styles himself a 虛擬「國民檢察官」/虛擬「國民法官」
and spends the piece listing 證據鏈 gaps he wants 新北檢署 to close in the 黃逸豪 假車禍 prosecution — whether the
卡米地 潑糞 suspects are 到案 and whether they are the same people, which 通訊軟體 (LINE/WeChat/QQ) linked the five defendants
to the anonymous 「K」 and whether 截圖/通話錄音 exist, how 檢調 verified K is a 大陸人士 and where the IP/終端 was, who assigned
the codename 「K」 (he asks whether it stands for KMT and why not D or C), whether the five have 前科 or 出境 records, how the
NT$250,000 訂金 was paid and whether the 匯款帳號 can lead back to K, whether other motives (債務/合約/江湖恩怨/情侶仇恨/競爭者買凶)
were ruled out, why the defendants would 「避輕就重」 under 反滲透法 exposure, whether 黃逸豪's reach is wide enough to justify a
鎮壓 at all, the price incoherence (NT$450,000 to break someone's limbs vs the 矢板明夫 assault which produced no proven payment),
the 「溫柔」 of these 鎮壓 (the attackers 「善心大發」 and did not break the limbs; 矢板明夫 only lost a tooth), and his own
unsubstantiated 「自導自演」 hunch — all carried here as **the columnist's questions and opinions, attributed, no finding of fact**.
The only news content is the recap of the 新北地檢署 2026-10-21 偵結 already on this spine (「K」 NT$450,000 報酬, 鄭博軒 蒐集個資,
then 黃家慶/游幃捷/范崇豪/廖本城, GPS on the 機車 in Jul–Aug, 假車禍 2026-09-11, 尾款 refused, 求刑 5y6m/5y/4y/3y6m/2y7m,
重傷害未遂 + 違反個資法) and the 陸委會 譴責 — plus one **quoted official line** from 國安局長 **蔡明彥** (via 中央社 photo caption):
中共跨境鎮壓, 已掌握黑幫與徵信社名單. The closing question 「為什麼跨境鎮壓抓得到，跨境電詐抓不到?」 is the columnist's rhetorical
frame; 「跨境電詐」/「電詐」 is a **true zero** in the vault, i.e. no fraud-side record exists to hang it on.

**Tier ruling.** **Tier D — cite reporting, no page mint.** CONTROL §1b discriminator is party-status, and every limb of the

is a party to it, and the columnist's skepticism (「自導自演」) is his, not an archive finding.

— needs-tier t_02c71bb5 (**4th publisher surface of one 方瑋立 article**). 放言科技傳媒「放．新聞 / 快訊」, 文／放言編輯部 **葛瑞貞**, 2026.10.24 15:57pm, `fountmedia.io/article/437697`. **Body read in full** in-browser from the live 放言 URL — the Google News redirect resolved cleanly this run (`news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiUkFVX3lxTFBBOHVD…?oc=5` → `fountmedia.io/article/437697`), so the ruling is on read text, not on the wire headline (the t_88e678ef rule). **Form: a 快訊 rewrite that cites its own source.** The piece opens on 許凱傑's 10-23 受訪 and states 「根據《自由時報》報導」 — it is 放言's republication of the 自由時報 copy already canonical on this spine (方瑋立, 10-23, 紙本 `news.ltn.com.tw/news/politics/paper/1775758` / 網路 `breakingnews/5616967`, syndicated to LINE TODAY `JPyr767`), not a fresh interview. Substance retained, all of it 許凱傑's as reported: 防制跨國壓迫 cannot rest on 提高刑度 alone — 「及早預警、追查幕後、保護被害人」 must run together so the state can intervene **before** harm; 加拿大's 2024 反外國干預立法 expanded CSIS's authority to disclose threat information to non-federal partners under safeguards (cited to 加拿大公共安全部 official material), and 澳洲's first foreign-interference prosecution invokes 《刑法》 §92.4 for 準備／策劃 foreign interference, i.e. enforcement need not wait for the interference to succeed (cited to 澳洲聯邦檢察署 published case); 現行《反滲透法》 leaves 補強空間 for 滲透背景的恐嚇、強制、非法拘禁 and 非法蒐集／利用個資; liability should be assessed **separately** for 境外指揮者 / 中間聯絡人 / 在地執行者 by 知情程度與參與情形; 美、德 experience should inform integrating 警察、資安與情報機關 information while protecting 情報來源 and keeping evidence 可受法庭檢驗; and a 通報、風險評估與保護 flow so victims fearing retaliation against 本人或境外家屬 dare to seek help. On the legislative boundary: the 境外勢力 relationship and the specific 違法行為 must be defined so no one is presumed guilty from 政治立場或兩岸往來 — 「防衛性民主不是降低法治標準，而是讓民主國家有能力透過嚴謹的法律程序，防止威權國家利用民主社會的開放性來壓迫人民。」 立委 **王定宇** adds the 修法 angle: punishing only the surface acts (偷拍、非法蒐集個資、傷害) misses the 境外指使與政治恐嚇 purpose, so 受中共指使 should be a sentencing aggravator, and he is pushing legislation on 網路「抖內」洗錢 and 受中國黨政軍授權、資助的網路霸凌／輿論操作. **Labeling:** every characterization above is the named speaker's, attributed; no indictment, filing, or court finding is cited in this item and nothing here is recorded as established fact.

### 2026-10-23 — 報導，尚未成為我們的檔案

**Reported.** 2026-10-23 — 反制中共「跨國鎮壓」 國安法官許凱傑：預警、查幕後、保護被害人同步並進 [自由電子報 via LINE TODAY](https://today.line.me/tw/v3/article/JPyr767)
([wire](https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiVkFVX3lxTFBoT09kZEMyVFF2SVNBZzIxeXlPRE8zV1pRZUdUWTQ4NFNfc2ZGcG9mTnYwS3JBdElLb19GSWtDZGk4THAyNXJ3NjFtdTFFOXdocEQwcWtR?oc=5))
— 自由時報記者方瑋立台北報導, syndicated to LINE TODAY. **Body read in full** from the live LINE TODAY article URL
(the Google News redirect is JS-only and web_extract returned "Content was inaccessible or not found" — the
t_88e678ef read-text rule; redirect resolved in-browser to `today.line.me/tw/v3/article/JPyr767`). The item is the
**legislative-expertise leg** of the 2026 legislative-response cycle already carried on this spine (t_88e678ef
律師修法 track, t_bdebf07d 民進黨/國民黨 攻防 track): 台北地方法院國安專庭法官、國防院特約研究員 **許凱傑** 受訪 argues
防制跨國壓迫 must pair 提高刑度 with 「及早預警、追查幕後、保護被害人」 — cites 加拿大 (intelligence agencies may lawfully
warn individuals/institutions) and 澳洲 (preparation/planning of foreign interference, and funding a foreign
intelligence agency, are punishable before an attack), urges 依境外指揮者、中間聯絡人及在地執行者分別認定責任, 參考美、德
整合警察、資安與情報機關資訊, and 通報、評估風險與保護流程 so victims fearing retaliation against 本人或境外家屬 can seek help;
warns 修法須清楚界定境外勢力關係與具體違法行為, 「防衛性民主不是降低法治標準」. 民進黨立委 **王定宇** 連署提出「反滲透法」與「國家安全法」
修正草案: 納管網路「抖內」洗錢與代操網路霸凌/輿論操作（受中國黨政軍授權、資助）, 比照「組織犯罪防制條例」精神加重刑責, 稱中國為
「最大的犯罪組織、最大的黑幫」; the named underlying cases (徵信社偷拍 沈伯洋 住處含幼童面容、黑幫打人) are the prosecutions
already on this spine.

**Tier ruling.** **Tier D — cite reporting, no page mint.** The community is not a party to this item: every
speaker is a Taiwanese official or legislator acting in Taipei, the legislation is ROC domestic law
(反滲透法 / 國家安全法 amendments, 組織犯罪防制條例 analogy), and the victim cases it names are tried in Taiwanese courts
(新北地檢署 黃逸豪案, 中檢 矢板明夫毆打案, 北檢 卡米地潑糞案 — all already on this spine). No 台美人 is targeted in this
item, no action occurs on U.S./Canadian soil against anyone we hold, and the 境外家屬 concern is a general
victim-protection policy point, not a families-surveilled-here claim about our community. CONTROL §1b: the
discriminator is party-status, not theme — the theme is ours (this page's standing Tier E designation covers the
pattern), this item is not.

### 2021 — "獵捕" of Taiwanese worldwide

**Sourced.** [強制送中，國際組織：中國在全世界「獵捕」台灣人](../articles/taiwanjustice-net/2021/20211202002920_2021_11_30_強制送中_國際組織_中國在全世界_獵捕_台灣_b8f2774523ae4382.md)
records international organizations' finding that Taiwan nationals are pursued abroad. The
framing is not "influence" but *capture*.

### 2022 — religious communities targeted under 天網 / 獵狐

**Sourced.** [中國通過"天網""獵狐"跨國鎮壓宗教團體成員](../articles/taiwanjustice-net/2022/20220706130750_2022_05_12_中國通過天網獵狐跨國鎮壓宗教團體成員_eb63ef42380dfc35.md)
— the operation against religious-group members abroad, filed under the 天網/獵狐 framework;
the archive's own title records 美國際宗教自由委員會 naming 習近平 in its criticism.

### 2023 — the legislative response begins

**Sourced.** [美兩黨參議員提案打擊中國跨境鎮壓行動](../articles/taiwanjustice-net/2023/20230322190637_2023_03_17_美兩黨參議員提案打擊中國跨境鎮壓行動_826d63f392b7d3a0.md)
— bipartisan Senate action, i.e. this is a recognized state-to-state problem, not an
immigrant-community rumor.

**Sourced.** [加拿大警方稱已關閉與"中國警察站"有關的"非法警務活動"](../articles/taiwanjustice-net/2023/20230922225159_2023_06_01_加拿大警方稱已關閉與中國警察站有關的非_77a87ed49e934a1d.md)
— Toronto. Physical police stations operated on Canadian soil.

**Sourced.** [「不支持言論自由，就別來上學」普渡大學校長公開信](../articles/taiwanjustice-net/2023/20230922210719_2021_12_16_不支持言論自由_就別來上學_-普渡大學校長_4bfbf4da223746b9.md)
— the campus vector: students harassing peers over political speech, with the pressure traced
to PRC-aligned organizing.

### 2024 — sanctions as the countermeasure; retaliation against individuals

**Sourced.** [許穎婷推制裁法案觸怒港府 遭懸賞百萬通緝](../articles/taiwanjustice-net/2024/20240425070811_root_49d7572446be0142.md)
— a legislator of our region facing a bounty. This is the community as a party in the most
literal sense.

**Sourced.** [習近平訪美期間許多抗議人士被圍毆](../articles/taiwanjustice-net/2024/20240425142946_root_ee94dde3970d7da5.md)
— physical assault on protesters during a state visit; human-rights groups sought hearings.

### 2025 — the multilateral layer

**Sourced.** [跨國調查揭北京鎮壓滲透全球 聯合國淪中國打壓異己工具](../articles/taiwanjustice-net/2025/20250521215649_跨國調查揭北京鎮壓滲透全球-聯合國淪中國打壓異_d15bd4c73c6173fe.md)
— an investigative body of work, not one outlet's account.

**Sourced.** [香港通過基本法23條立法 跨國近80位政界人士譴責](../articles/taiwanjustice-net/2025/20250328051024_香港通過基本法23條立法-跨國近80位政界人士譴責_4321405224b77bb1.md)

**Sourced.** [區域宗教自由論壇：台灣是中共遂行「再教育」下一目標](../articles/taiwanjustice-net/2025/20251012110806_區域宗教自由論壇-美國人權專家_台灣是中共遂行_2fb6fededc47fcaa.md)

**Sourced.** [美國務院報告：中共戰狼及假帳號充斥網絡空間](../articles/taiwanjustice-net/2025/20251016133115_美國務院報告_中共戰狼及假帳號充斥網絡空間_63dc3c01d786f049.md)
— the information-operation layer that precedes and accompanies physical action.

## NEED YOU

The archive's coverage of this theme is real but **thin between 2021 and 2023**, and absent on
the 2026 legislative cycle. If you were in Toronto, Los Angeles, or a campus community during
any of these operations — your first-hand account is the primary source we do not have. One
sentence is enough.
